  A packet capture driver ships with PktTrack network tools.
  An internal audit flagged a race condition in the capture
  commit path. Management said the window is "too small to
  exploit in practice." Prove them wrong.

  You have been given:  WinCapture.sys

  Goal:
    1. Reverse WinCapture.sys to understand the IOCTL interface.
    2. Find and exploit the race condition in IOCTL_COMMIT_CAPTURE.
    3. Write exploit.exe (x64 Windows PE) that wins the race,
       corrupts the key object, and retrieves the flag.
    4. Send exploit.exe here -- the server runs it against a live
       WinCapture driver instance and returns the output.

  Driver interface:  \\.\pipe\WinCapture
  Protocol:          See WinCapture.sys (binary IOCTL over named pipe)

  Send your exploit.exe as base64.
  Paste it line by line, then send a line containing only: END
